<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>重要</strong>:</div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">将 <code>apps\api\.env.windows</code> 重命名为 <code>apps\api\.env</code></li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">确保文件在 <code>apps\api</code> 目录下</li>
</ul>

[&amp;&gt;*:first-child&gt;*:first-child]:pt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child&gt;*:first-child]:pb-0"><div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] 设计和动画效果实现完成！</h2></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words">我已经成功为根页面和 /home 页面的新建任务功能增加了完整的 UX 设计，实现了对话页面样式变更和流式消息增量显示，所有代码已推送到 GitHub。</div></div>

<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%E2%9C%85%E5%B7%B2%E5%AE%9E%E7%8E%B0%E7%9A%84%E6%A0%B8%E5%BF%83%E5%8A%9F%E8%83%BD" style="line-height: 1.3;">✅ 已实现的核心功能</h2></div>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="1%E5%8F%8C%E6%A8%A1%E5%BC%8F%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1" style="line-height: 1.3;">1. 双模式设计</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>初始输入模式</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">居中的大输入框，Swiss International Style 设计风格</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">品牌 Logo 和欢迎语</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">4 个快速操作按钮（预设任务模板）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">完整的工具栏（附件、连接器、模型选择、语音输入）</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>对话模式</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">对话消息列表（用户消息右对齐，AI 消息左对齐）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">流式消息显示（打字机效果，每字符 20ms）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">固定在底部的输入框</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自动滚动到最新消息</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="2%E6%B5%81%E7%95%85%E7%9A%84%E5%8A%A8%E7%94%BB%E6%95%88%E6%9E%9C" style="line-height: 1.3;">2. 流畅的动画效果</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>页面切换动画</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">使用 framer-motion 实现淡入淡出</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">初始模式到对话模式的平滑过渡（300ms）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">AnimatePresence 管理组件进出</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>消息动画</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">用户消息从右侧滑入（x: 20 → 0）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">AI 消息从左侧滑入（x: -20 → 0）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">每条消息独立的进入动画</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>输入框动画</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">对话模式下从底部滑入（y: 100 → 0）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">延迟 200ms 开始，持续 400ms</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">使用 easeOut 缓动函数</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>打字机效果</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">AI 响应的流式文本显示</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">逐字符渲染，自然的阅读体验</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自动触发滚动</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="3%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%9A%84%E6%B6%88%E6%81%AF%E7%B1%BB%E5%9E%8B%E6%94%AF%E6%8C%81" style="line-height: 1.3;">3. 完整的消息类型支持</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>用户消息</strong> - 深色气泡，右对齐<br>
<strong>AI 消息</strong> - 浅色卡片，显示 Agent 名称，流式显示<br>
<strong>系统消息</strong> - 连接状态、处理中指示器<br>
<strong>澄清问题</strong> - 蓝色卡片，支持选项按钮和文本输入<br>
<strong>错误提示</strong> - 红色卡片<br>
<strong>成功提示</strong> - 绿色卡片</div></div>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="4websocket%E5%AE%9E%E6%97%B6%E9%80%9A%E4%BF%A1" style="line-height: 1.3;">4. WebSocket 实时通信</h3></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">集成 <code>useTaskCreationAgent</code> Hook</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自动连接管理和重连</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">实时消息推送</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">状态同步（连接、处理、消息、澄清问题）</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="5%E7%94%A8%E6%88%B7%E4%BD%93%E9%AA%8C%E4%BC%98%E5%8C%96" style="line-height: 1.3;">5. 用户体验优化</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>自动滚动</strong> - 新消息自动滚动到底部<br>
<strong>快速操作</strong> - 点击预设模板快速开始<br>
<strong>键盘快捷键</strong> - Enter 发送，Shift+Enter 换行<br>
<strong>加载状态</strong> - 连接中和处理中的视觉反馈<br>
<strong>响应式设计</strong> - 适配不同屏幕尺寸</div></div>

<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="4%E8%AE%BF%E9%97%AE%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8" style="line-height: 1.3;">4. 访问应用</h3></div>
<ul>

</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="5%E6%B5%8B%E8%AF%95%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B" style="line-height: 1.3;">5. 测试流程</h3></div>
<ol>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>输入模式测试</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">查看居中的输入框设计</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">点击快速操作按钮</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">测试工具栏按钮</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>发送消息</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">输入任务描述</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">点击发送按钮</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">观察页面切换动画</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>对话模式测试</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">查看消息显示效果</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">观察打字机效果</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">测试自动滚动</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>澄清问题交互</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">等待 AI 提问</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">点击选项或输入回答</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">继续对话流程</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>

<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%F0%9F%93%9Dgit%E6%8F%90%E4%BA%A4%E4%BF%A1%E6%81%AF" style="line-height: 1.3;">📝 Git 提交信息</h2></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>分支</strong>: <code>task-creation-agent</code><br>
<strong>最新提交</strong>: <code>4cd85ed</code><br>
<strong>提交信息</strong>: "feat: 实现任务创建智能体的 UX 设计和动画效果"</div></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>变更内容</strong>:</div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">为 HomePage 和 Home 页面添加对话模式</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">实现流式消息显示（打字机效果 ）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">添加页面切换动画和消息进入动画</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">集成 WebSocket 实时通信</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">实现自动滚动和澄清问题交互</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">优化响应式设计和用户体验</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">更新 Vite 配置以支持外部访问</li>
</ul>

<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%F0%9F%8E%AC%E9%A2%84%E6%9C%9F%E6%95%88%E6%9E%9C" style="line-height: 1.3;">🎬 预期效果</h2></div>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="%E5%88%9D%E5%A7%8B%E7%8A%B6%E6%80%81" style="line-height: 1.3;">初始状态</h3></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">页面加载后显示优雅的居中输入框</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">Logo 和标题带有淡入动画</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">快速操作按钮整齐排列</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="%E5%8F%91%E9%80%81%E6%B6%88%E6%81%AF%E5%90%8E" style="line-height: 1.3;">发送消息后</h3></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">输入框平滑向上移动并缩小</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">对话区域从上方淡入</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">用户消息从右侧滑入</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">底部输入框从下方滑入</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="ai%E5%93%8D%E5%BA%94%E6%97%B6" style="line-height: 1.3;">AI 响应时</h3></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">AI 消息从左侧滑入</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">Agent 名称显示在消息上方</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">文本以打字机效果逐字显示</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">页面自动滚动跟随新消息</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="%E6%BE%84%E6%B8%85%E9%97%AE%E9%A2%98%E6%97%B6" style="line-height: 1.3;">澄清问题时</h3></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">蓝色卡片从左侧滑入</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">选项按钮整齐排列</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">点击后立即响应</li>
</ul>

<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%F0%9F%93%8B%E5%AE%9E%E7%8E%B0%E6%A6%82%E8%A7%88" style="line-height: 1.3;">📋 实现概览</h2></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words">已完成根页面和 /home 页面的新建任务功能的 UX 设计，实现了对话页面样式变更和流式消息增量显示，包括平滑的动画效果。</div></div>
<div node="[object Object]" data-hr="true" class="h-[13px] my-[1px] flex items-center"><div class="w-full h-[1px] bg-[var(--border-main)]"></div></div>
<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%E2%9C%85%E5%B7%B2%E5%AE%9E%E7%8E%B0%E7%9A%84%E5%8A%9F%E8%83%BD" style="line-height: 1.3;">✅ 已实现的功能</h2></div>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="1%E9%A1%B5%E9%9D%A2%E6%A8%A1%E5%BC%8F%E5%88%87%E6%8D%A2" style="line-height: 1.3;">1. 页面模式切换</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>初始输入模式 (Input Mode)</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">居中的大输入框设计</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">品牌标识和欢迎语</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">快速操作按钮</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">工具栏（附件、连接器、模型选择、语音输入）</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>对话模式 (Chat Mode)</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">对话消息列表</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">流式消息显示（打字机效果）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">固定在底部的输入框</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">平滑的模式切换动画</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="2%E5%8A%A8%E7%94%BB%E6%95%88%E6%9E%9C" style="line-height: 1.3;">2. 动画效果</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>页面切换动画</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>framer-motion</code> 实现的淡入淡出效果</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">初始输入模式到对话模式的平滑过渡</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">持续时间: 300ms</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>消息动画</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">用户消息从右侧滑入</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">AI 消息从左侧滑入</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">每条消息独立的进入动画</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>打字机效果</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">AI 消息的流式显示</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">每个字符 20ms 的延迟</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自然的阅读体验</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>输入框动画</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">对话模式下从底部滑入</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">延迟 200ms 开始，持续 400ms</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">使用 <code>easeOut</code> 缓动函数</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="3%E6%B6%88%E6%81%AF%E7%B1%BB%E5%9E%8B%E6%94%AF%E6%8C%81" style="line-height: 1.3;">3. 消息类型支持</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>用户消息</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">右对齐</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">深色背景</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">简洁的圆角气泡</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>AI 消息</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">左对齐</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">浅色卡片背景</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">显示 Agent 名称（意图识别、任务规划、执行计划）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">流式文本显示</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>系统消息</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">连接状态提示</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">处理中指示器</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">错误提示（红色）</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">成功提示（绿色）</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>澄清问题</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">蓝色卡片背景</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">支持选项按钮</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">支持文本输入</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="4websocket%E9%9B%86%E6%88%90" style="line-height: 1.3;">4. WebSocket 集成</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>实时通信</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">使用 <code>useTaskCreationAgent</code> Hook</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自动连接管理</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">消息队列处理</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>状态管理</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>isConnected</code> - 连接状态</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>isProcessing</code> - 处理状态</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>messages</code> - 消息列表</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>currentQuestion</code> - 当前澄清问题</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="5%E5%93%8D%E5%BA%94%E5%BC%8F%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1" style="line-height: 1.3;">5. 响应式设计</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>容器宽度</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">最大宽度: 3xl (48rem)</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自动居中</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">左右内边距: 1.5rem</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>消息宽度</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">最大宽度: 80%</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自动换行</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">保持可读性</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="6%E7%94%A8%E6%88%B7%E4%BD%93%E9%AA%8C%E4%BC%98%E5%8C%96" style="line-height: 1.3;">6. 用户体验优化</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>自动滚动</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">新消息自动滚动到底部</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">平滑滚动效果</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">使用 <code>messagesEndRef</code> 实现</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>快速操作</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">4 个预设的任务模板</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">点击即可快速开始</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自动切换到对话模式</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>键盘快捷键</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">Enter 发送消息</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">Shift + Enter 换行</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>加载状态</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">连接中提示</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">处理中指示器</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">旋转的加载图标</li>
</ul>
<div node="[object Object]" data-hr="true" class="h-[13px] my-[1px] flex items-center"><div class="w-full h-[1px] bg-[var(--border-main)]"></div></div>
<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%F0%9F%8E%A8%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E9%A3%8E%E6%A0%BC" style="line-height: 1.3;">🎨 设计风格</h2></div>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="swissinternationalstyle%2Bdigitalminimalism" style="line-height: 1.3;">Swiss International Style + Digital Minimalism</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>核心原则</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">清晰的视觉层次</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">一致的输入体验</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">最小化的视觉干扰</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">功能优先的设计</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>颜色系统</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">前景色: <code>foreground</code></li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">背景色: <code>background</code></li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">边框色: <code>border</code></li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">静音色: <code>muted-foreground</code></li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">强调色: <code>primary</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>圆角设计</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">输入框: 24px (<code>rounded-3xl</code>)</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">按钮: 12px (<code>rounded-xl</code>)</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">消息气泡: 16px (<code>rounded-2xl</code>)</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">卡片: 8px (<code>rounded-lg</code>)</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>阴影层次</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">输入框: <code>shadow-lg</code></li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">悬停: <code>shadow-xl</code></li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">卡片: 默认阴影</li>
</ul>
<div node="[object Object]" data-hr="true" class="h-[13px] my-[1px] flex items-center"><div class="w-full h-[1px] bg-[var(--border-main)]"></div></div>
<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%F0%9F%93%82%E6%96%87%E4%BB%B6%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84" style="line-height: 1.3;">📂 文件结构</h2></div>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="%E6%9B%B4%E6%96%B0%E7%9A%84%E6%96%87%E4%BB%B6" style="line-height: 1.3;">更新的文件</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>1. HomePage.tsx</strong> (<code>apps/web/client/src/pages/HomePage.tsx</code>)</div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">实现了完整的对话模式</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">集成 WebSocket 通信</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">添加动画效果</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>2. Home.tsx</strong> (<code>apps/web/client/src/pages/Home.tsx</code>)</div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">与 HomePage 相同的实现</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">支持从根页面跳转</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">相同的动画效果</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>3. vite.config.ts</strong> (<code>apps/web/vite.config.ts</code>)</div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">更新文件系统配置</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">添加 HMR 配置</li>
</ul>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84" style="line-height: 1.3;">组件结构</h3></div>

<div node="[object Object]" data-hr="true" class="h-[13px] my-[1px] flex items-center"><div class="w-full h-[1px] bg-[var(--border-main)]"></div></div>
<div class="mt-[1.4em] mb-[1px]" data-level="2"><h2 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.5em]" data-anchor="%F0%9F%94%A7%E6%8A%80%E6%9C%AF%E5%AE%9E%E7%8E%B0" style="line-height: 1.3;">🔧 技术实现</h2></div>
<div class="mt-[1em] mb-[1px]" data-level="3"><h3 class="font-[600] py-[3px] text-[1.25em]" data-anchor="%E4%BD%BF%E7%94%A8%E7%9A%84%E5%BA%93" style="line-height: 1.3;">使用的库</h3></div>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>UI 组件</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>@radix-ui/*</code> - 无障碍的 UI 组件</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>lucide-react</code> - 图标库</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot"><code>framer-motion</code> - 动画库</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>状态管理</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">React Hooks (<code>useState</code>, <code>useRef</code>, <code>useEffect</code>)</li>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">自定义 Hook (<code>useTaskCreationAgent</code>)</li>
</ul>
<div class="my-[1px]"><div class="py-[3px] whitespace-pre-wrap u-break-words"><strong>样式</strong></div></div>
<ul>
<li class="u-break-words ps-[2px] hide-loading-dot">Tailwind CSS</li>
